# Complete LMDB native execution support and eliminate SPARQLoscope timeouts

This ExecPlan implements the user's accepted plan of 2026-10-21. It is a living document maintained according to root `PLANS.md`. Keep Progress, Surprises & Discoveries, Decision Log, and Outcomes & Retrospective current. Preserve the accepted semantics, performance targets, and all original evidence when recording implementation refinements.

## Purpose / Big Picture


Make RDF4J's LMDB query engine execute the missing native capabilities exposed by SPARQLoscope without sacrificing SPARQL correctness. All 105 full-data AUTO queries must finish within 90 seconds on the existing benchmark host, retaining the configured 180-second timeout. AUTO means ordinary automatic strategy selection. Explicitly forced strategies certify capability and correctness, but a deliberately inefficient forced algorithm is not promised the same performance. It must cancel reliably.

Use the existing 503,865,752-triple, format-6, bulk-loaded DBLP store. Keep the sketch-based estimator and context-pair sketches disabled. Retain Java 26, current memory budgets, other enabled native features, canonical query text, HTTP protocol, full result consumption, and existing timing definitions. No query-ID/text matching, synthetic calibration, weakened assertions, or increased timeouts are allowed.

## Surprises & Discoveries


The original canonical HTTP run completed 104 queries; GROUP_CONCAT timed out at 203.397 seconds. The strategy investigation found eleven additional 180-second-capped failures: nine distinct-subject counting executions and two GROUP_CONCAT executions. The largest elapsed time was 524.422 seconds, an overshoot of 344.422 seconds. Those raw records have no internal phase timings, so a particular merge/sort/spill attribution is not established.

Source review established a cancellation dependency: ordinary kernels receive non-null cancellation tokens, but those tokens do not directly observe QueryExecutionDeadline. Expiration schedules delegate close, which can be deferred while an aggregate is active; close-driven cancellation can therefore wait for the operation it should interrupt. Comments claiming ordinary tokens are null are stale. Long group merge, aggregate set-union, result drain and packed-sort loops also need polling.

The string functions already have PURE registrations. Missing recursive scalar typing makes certain nested expressions fall back to semantic-value closures and serial grouping. Negative/sticky predicate masks also conflate placement with worker replay/ownership capability. SAMPLE/GROUP_CONCAT currently use generic row state rejected by parallel and packed reducers.

The initial required root clean install completed successfully in 01:03. Full output is preserved in `benchmark-runs/sparqloscope-20261020/implementation-evidence/initial-build/maven-build.log`; this is build evidence, not a correctness test. The native evaluation lifetime owner is `NativeExecutionContext`, and long-loop group merging lives in `LmdbNativeGroupTable` and `LmdbNativeAggregateState` rather than only `LmdbNativeGroupStep`.

Fresh first-red evidence now exists. `TimeLimitIterationCancellationTest#deadlineRequestsCancellationBeforeClosingActiveAggregate` ran one test with one failure: cancellation was not signalled before deferred close. `NativeCancellationTokenDeadlineTest` ran three tests with three failures (captured worker deadline, expired-before-creation, nested scope). `LmdbNativeScalarPlanTest#nestedScalarConversionsBindCompositionalWorkerRecipes` ran one test with one failure: STRLEN over STRBEFORE compiled to null. Exact reports, XML and verify logs are retained under `implementation-evidence/deadline-first-red/` and `implementation-evidence/scalar-first-red/`, and compact evidence is appended to root `initial-evidence.txt` before later report replacement.

The recursive compiler bridge is verified: the scalar-plan class passed20tests and a real-LMDB aggregate test passed all five nested string/Boolean/IF/COALESCE query variants against the generic evaluator while asserting that both generated and interpreted parallel schedulers actually ran. Reports are in `implementation-evidence/scalar-green/` and `implementation-evidence/parallel-aggregate-green/`. The common cancellation class now passed16tests, captured-token tests passed3tests, aggregate terminal/set-union/packed-sort/JFR phase cancellation passed6tests, and packed worker failure/drain passed4tests. The final common-close race, cancellation/cleanup error precedence and JFR phase marker tests have separately captured first-red reports under `explicit-close-first-red/`, `cleanup-precedence-first-red/` and `phase-first-red/`; their matching greens are retained under `cancellation-final-green/`.

Fresh worker-predicate tests failed2unit cases (no EXISTS worker recipe; sticky placement erased replay safety) and1real-LMDB route test (the generated parallel scheduler stayed at0). Their reports are in `implementation-evidence/predicate-first-red/`. The new shared binder is being extracted from packed execution and predicates will use its independent filter, codec and computed-value ownership; lexical subquery producers still need explicit worker construction before they can be admitted.

The core correlated worker regression now passes all five EXISTS/NOT EXISTS/UNION/OPTIONAL/MINUS query variants against the generic evaluator, with actual generated and interpreted parallel scheduler counters increasing. Specialized cached/ordered comparison filters bypassed ordinary scalar compilation, so they needed an immutable structural proof wrapper that preserves their existing fast path and lowering identity; manually supplied opaque comparisons remain unproved. Exact green reports are in `implementation-evidence/scoped-cancellation-snapshot-3/parallel-worker-green/`. NativeSubqueryPlan previously memoized partial rows or returned exhausted input on cancellation; both matching regression methods now pass with terminal cancellation. The same snapshot ran25tests,2failures,0errors: fresh scoped-parent inheritance and post-sort spill polling reproductions failed before their fixes, with first-red reports under `nested-scope-first-red/` and `spill-poll-first-red/`. A coherent next snapshot adds explicit worker construction for native lexical subqueries/GRAPH, while kernel-hosted plan producers remain guarded until their separate first-red gate is observed.

Snapshot6 (`logs/mvnf/20261021-085012-verify.log`) passed17row cancellation tests and3actual parallel row-worker tests covering lexical predicates, recursive scalar BIND and composed EXISTS. A whole lexical root still requires an exact partition; it must never be evaluated in full independently by every worker merely to report parallel execution. Its diagnostic test exposed an internal zero-placeholder-to-unbound normalization hook with no construction proof. That source-independent slot conversion is being given an explicit internal builtin recipe, preserving the conservative gate for ordinary opaque closures. A variable-GRAPH/subquery case and boundary-binding counters will distinguish actual lexical construction from constant GRAPH rewrites.

The uncovered DISTINCT patch was removed before obtaining this corrected red, then restored. Runtime spilling, query-ledger cache ownership and typed/spillable result infrastructure are now in progress.

Only result-size-xlarge has AUTO candidates restricted to batch/nestedLoop; its single-pattern scan has no join-fusion opportunity. FactorizedTail was not offered for the selected shapes. A packed dispatch tag does not prove compressed join execution, and a filter-rejection ratio is not join compression.

## Decision Log


2026-10-21, user: Require fast AUTO and complete genuine capabilities, not fast execution of every deliberately forced algorithm. Require each AUTO query under 90 seconds while preserving the 180-second timeout. Keep sketches disabled.

2026-10-21, accepted design: Use explicit FULL_VALUE versus LEXICAL_LENGTH aggregate demands, rather than replacing the runtime String representation broadly. Preserve full values wherever any consumer needs them. Do not remove natural strategy exclusions.

2026-10-21, execution: Work in the current checkout without changing branch or publishing. Preserve unrelated tracked and untracked files. A Sol 6.1 xhigh coordinator owns integration and all operational handoffs; complex changes use Sol workers and routine complete build/test/benchmark workflows use Luna Max. Serialize Maven, formatting, and timed store access. Do not reuse workers idle more than 30 minutes.

2026-10-21, implementation refinement: Aggregate result summaries use an internal physical-result channel with RDF_TERM versus LEXICAL_SUMMARY cells and explicit bound/error state. They must never be fake Literals, numeric replacements for string bindings, BindingSet values or term IDs. Native aliases/projections/subqueries preserve the typed cell; a native STRLEN consumer reads the code-point length directly. Generic/full-value observers force FULL_VALUE during demand analysis. This avoids getLabel/internComputedValue/RdfTermKey contract violations. Ordered grouping streams complete groups through that channel; independently replayed subqueries use ledger-backed bounded/spillable replay rather than an all-groups List. Unknown transfer functions, equality/DISTINCT/order/group/filter/correlation consumers and uncertain lexical edges remain conservative. IF/COALESCE need explicit typed transfer rules before summaries cross them. Rationale: retaining ordinary RDF value contracts is required even when intermediate bindings are not publicly projected.

2026-10-21, reviewed demand design: Key demands and alias edges by lexical definition identity, never variable spelling. Capture provenance before rewrites and transfer it to clones; uncertain mappings force FULL_VALUE. NativeResultCursor carries cancellation and its resource owner through replay/spill close. RowState's typed summary lane must preserve boundness, trail rollback and worker/fork ownership; ordinary BindingSet, render, interning and RDF-term equality adapters reject escaped summaries. Tests cover shadowed subqueries, full observers, empty/error-only groups, exact Unicode lengths and separator contributions after DISTINCT. IF/COALESCE remain FULL_VALUE until explicit transfer rules are tested.

2026-10-21, reviewed physical-boundary refinement: Reuse NativeValuePositionAnalysis's immutable metadata and copySubtree transport for demand provenance rather than adding a parallel thread-local metadata system. Existing long-only IR PlanRows/hooks cannot transport summaries as UNKNOWN, numeric sentinels or RDF IDs. Keep a summary producer, native aliases and typed scalar consumers inside a proved native physical boundary; export ordinary RDF IDs after consumption. A whole lexical root retains its independent partition proof requirement. Explain must distinguish the native boundary from the legal surrounding IR path. Verify every optimizer/worker clone used by accepted workloads preserves metadata; uncertain mappings force FULL_VALUE.

2026-10-21, replay ownership refinement: A producer or spool cached inside NativeExecutionContext.nativeState must not persistently retain that same context, because state cleanup follows lease drainage. Borrowed producers retain the exact owner only while advancing; accepted readers retain it for their lifetime. Producer advancement is serialized independently from cancellation signalling. Publish complete rows atomically after successful append/spill, retain terminal failure/cancellation, and publish successful EOF only after actual producer exhaustion and a cancellation check. Later readers must not replay a successful prefix after terminal failure. Test two readers, early close, spill transition, failure after an emitted row and context close during active advancement.

2026-10-21, reviewed term-domain law refinement: A reusable recipe key includes all RDF inputs, bound/error outcomes and query-scope or lexical-frame identity whenever the recipe captures scope. PURE alone does not prove input independence. Weighted collapse requires explicit reducer laws covering promotion and heterogeneous errors as well as arithmetic; floating sums and representative-sensitive extrema retain ordered replay without stronger proof. Prove actual expression-evaluation reduction and exact multiplicities for repeated tuples, multiple group keys, context/inference visibility, generated terms and snapshot changes. Reuse exact visible rows and bounded tuple grouping; whole-dictionary counts are never a substitute.

2026-10-21, integration ownership refinement: A bounded probe's mutable lexical replay is keyed by the actual ProbeDeadline identity as well as its subquery/frame identity. Ordinary execution retains its ordinary key; workers inherit the same attempt object. Rejecting one trial must not poison ordinary or sibling replay with the trial's private deadline. An owning attempt disposes unfinished states only after accepted execution and readers have unwound, using compare-remove ownership and existing cleanup precedence. Inherited scopes never dispose shared attempt state. Genuine query cancellation and producer/source failures remain terminal within their evaluation. Published streaming results remain query-owned rather than being closed at scope restoration.

2026-10-21, reviewed physical dominance refinement: A legal proposal may carry a paired physical-work proof independently of scalar estimates and completed timing confidence. The initial implementation recognizes only an actual single non-wildcard EnumerateAdjKeys producer over a complete visible predicate header, unrestricted canonical Pattern input, global COUNT demand and exact nonempty visible cardinality. Its competing adjacency reduction must prove every channel can read that same predicate/direction header without opening rows; opposite-direction DISTINCT, context/range/group/HAVING and additional producer/filter shapes remain unknown. Record proof text and retain dominated proposals as legal inventory, explicit forced routes and fallbacks after the dominating opener declines. Do not alter the256cold band or fabricate large IR costs. Unknown shapes retain honest bounds and existing trials. Validate actual metadata counters and complete inventory together; per-timeout and paired full-data evidence remains separate.

Working directory: `/Users/havardottestad/Documents/Programming/rdf4j7`. Initial branch is develop and initial HEAD is `4f96df923d10c12e724a77dc0c24f65ecf07c739`. Initially tracked status is clean; `benchmark-runs/` is untracked and must be preserved.

Canonical evidence root is `benchmark-runs/sparqloscope-20261020/`. The completed store is `lmdb-store/`; the pinned original 105 queries are `upstream/benchmarks/dblp.benchmark.tsv`. Java options include Xmx32g, full adjacency budget 48GiB, value overlay 16GiB, retained overlay 24GiB and query memory 8GiB. The native evaluator, experimental query policy, value hash cache and compatible native opt-ins remain enabled. Check exported configuration rather than relying on these notes alone.

The selected CSV has twenty completed queries plus the supplemental failed GROUP_CONCAT. Cross-engine attachment times selected workloads only; hardware and graph parity are not established. Per-query client_time includes evaluation, JSON serialization/spooling, HTTP and transfer, but excludes later jq/YAML processing. Do not compare embedded times with HTTP times as if identical.

NativeCancellationToken, LmdbNativeGroupStep, LmdbNativeExpressionCompiler, LmdbNativeScalarExpressionCompiler, LmdbNativeFilters, LmdbNativePackedMorsels, LmdbNativeKernelLowering and the parallel/packed/grouping runtime implement the relevant behavior. Native storage and snapshot lifetimes are owned by LmdbStoreConnection and query sources. Test fixtures already cover deadlines, scalar plans, grouping, worker submission/draining, aggregate terms, EXISTS scopes, factorised tails, and arbitration.

A native scalar recipe is an immutable expression description that can be bound to a worker's local state. A term domain is the exact set of relevant input IDs/dependency tuples with their visible multiplicities. A reducer combines aggregate inputs and partial states. Factorisation avoids expanding repeated join combinations by carrying multiplicities; it is different from filtering rows or merely choosing a packed dispatcher.

## Plan of Work


### Milestone 1: Deadline propagation, polling and phase evidence


Write the smallest failing automated regression before production edits: an active aggregate blocks within hasNext entirely in memory, without storage calls, and deadline expiry must signal cancellation before inner close. Extend TimeLimitIteration cancellation and LMDB deadline/worker-drain fixtures, including expiration before registration, close races, nested scopes, and timeout/cleanup exception precedence.

On deadline expiry request cooperative cancellation before scheduling deferred closure. Capture the existing monotonic deadline in native evaluation scope and own its onExpiration registration. Propagate parent cancellation to worker tokens; ordinary kernel polls independently observe expiry, separately from probe deadlines. Close registrations with evaluation lifetime. Preserve accepted-task draining and native transaction/source ownership until workers are quiescent; release each lease and reservation exactly once. Cancellation is terminal: no candidate decline, fallback retry, or completed-runtime calibration.

Add bounded polling to group-table merging/results, aggregate set union, packed sorting and long inner loops. Update stale token comments. Add diagnostic phase markers for scan, expressions, grouping, DISTINCT, merge, spill, sort, render, cancellation request/acknowledgement and cleanup. Capture wall-clock and allocation profiles for both timed-out query shapes and representative serial/parallel failures, separately from unprofiled acceptance. The installed async-profiler resolver reported version4.4 under `/opt/homebrew/Cellar/async-profiler/4.4`; use the preinstalled macOS skill wrappers and verify availability again before use. Do not claim a particular recorded overshoot's phase without new evidence.

### Milestone 2: Compositional scalar compilation


Add an internal child-value compiler callback from the outer expression compiler to the scalar compiler. Retain specialized fast paths and use the callback for recursive string, numeric, Boolean and RDF-value conversions. Cover STRLEN over STRAFTER/STRBEFORE and casts over STRSTARTS/STRENDS, composed through IF/COALESCE and other supported expressions. Preserve errors, unbound behavior, short circuiting, explicit STR semantics, datatypes/languages, function effects and cycle avoidance. Reuse immutable NativeScalarPlan and existing worker binding. Do not add duplicate PURE registry entries or weaken the serial-safety gate.

### Milestone 3: Worker-local correlated predicates


Introduce immutable internal predicate capabilities separating correlation reads, effects, placement, replay safety, worker binding and query-scope requirements. Extract the recursive binder currently in LmdbNativePackedMorsels into shared infrastructure. Bind EXISTS/NOT EXISTS subplans and mutable memo/probe/codec/computed-value state separately per worker, sharing only immutable query scope and the same snapshot. Preserve lexical frames through OPTIONAL, MINUS, UNION, subqueries and nested correlation. Unproved or effectful predicates retain serial execution. Verify actual parallel workers and independent ownership, not merely candidate eligibility.

### Milestone 4: Typed, bounded aggregate reducers


Replace blanket generic-row-state rejection with shared reducer capabilities defining normalization, DISTINCT domain, accumulation, merge law, ordering, weighting and spill encoding. Keep primitive numeric fast paths. SAMPLE merges eligible representatives with membership semantics. GROUP_CONCAT uses bounded ordered segments and logical ordering metadata, not worker completion order. DISTINCT deduplicates exact RDF terms or complete mappings including boundness. Preserve arithmetic, promotion, errors, extrema representatives and existing ordering contracts. Arbitrary custom collectors stay serial without a trusted merge contract.

Reuse the query memory ledger and spill infrastructure. Spill generated-value payloads as well as IDs so runtime dictionaries do not hide unbounded retention. Stream groups when input order proves grouping order and consume completed groups downstream rather than retaining every group. Validate tiny budgets, skew, spills, close/failure paths and exact owner release.

### Milestone 5: Term-domain execution and aggregate demand


Introduce internal NativeTermDomainPlan for relevant IDs or complete dependency tuples with exact visible multiplicities. Derive domains from visible statement input under the current snapshot, context and inference settings, never the entire dictionary. Evaluate proven reusable lexical recipes once per key, apply legal weighted reductions, or use exact memberships/results while preserving original rows. Budget and spill through the query ledger. Preserve Unicode code points, complete IRI namespace/local-name values, language rules and normative regex flags/behavior; unsupported/effectful or non-collapsible expressions retain ordinary execution.

Add FULL_VALUE and LEXICAL_LENGTH aggregate-result demands through alias/def-use analysis. Length-only GROUP_CONCAT uses eligible code-point lengths plus separator contributions, with DISTINCT before summarization and the identical normal input/error/coercion semantics. Any full-value consumer requires FULL_VALUE; uncertain composition remains conservative. A native scalar consumer understands the summary; never replace the string binding with a numeric binding. Pair summary evaluation with ordered streaming grouping/outer consumption to avoid replacing strings with an equally unbounded group map.

Implement an internal physical result row/cell channel carrying RDF_TERM or LEXICAL_SUMMARY plus bound/error state. Preserve the summary cell through native aliases, projection and lexical subquery boundaries; keep summary-bearing frames separate from ordinary long term-ID slots until consumed, or add an explicitly typed lane with the same rollback/copy/worker lifetime rules. BindingSet/public RDF adapters accept only RDF_TERM cells. Summaries may never reach Value.getLabel(), stringValue(), term interning, RDF-term equality or rendering. Demand analysis must therefore account for every consumer, including aliases, full-value projection, equality, DISTINCT, ordering, grouping, filters and correlation; uncertain or generic edges force FULL_VALUE. Stream once-consumed native boundaries only when replay cardinality/lifetime is proved; otherwise use the query ledger and spillable replay.

Represent each lexical definition with identity, using variable names only to resolve references within that scope. Alias edges and outputs that merge alternative definitions propagate a demand to their source definitions; root projection, RDF-term comparisons, grouping, DISTINCT, correlation and unknown adapters force full values. Only an explicitly supported native STRLEN observation requests length. Aggregate-valued Extension clones mirror their authoritative Group binding rather than defining a second aggregate. Store the frozen GroupElem-to-demand map with NativeValuePositionAnalysis and transfer it through its existing subtree-copy mechanism. A missing or ambiguous mapping is FULL_VALUE. The new LmdbNativeAggregateDemandTest exercises alias chains, shadowed subqueries, full observers, Unicode separators, DISTINCT and error-only inputs. LmdbNativeTermDomainTest covers relevant graph-local terms/dependency tuples, duplicate outer VALUES weights and volatile per-solution values. These tests must first fail on the missing physical capability before demand/domain production edits.

M4 provides NativeResultCell/Row/Cursor and NativeResultReplay. Its current replay contract requires finish before opening readers; add a lazy producer/replay wrapper for the canonical once-consumed subquery so the first downstream consumer can stream completed groups while retaining bounded/spilled replay for probes or fallback. Serialize producer advancement, retain its source/cursor owner and lease, and publish completion only after real exhaustion with a cancellation check. A cancelled/failed producer must never turn a cached prefix into successful EOF. NativeResultStep and native projection/extension adapters may expose evaluateNative without converting through BindingSet first. Keep summary-bearing plans and typed consumers atomic at long-only kernel boundaries; the exported required columns must contain ordinary RDF IDs after summary consumption.

### Milestone 6: Common arbitration and legal factorisation


Build a common semantically legal candidate inventory and constrain it by execution mode. Single-authority plans still compare legal interpreted/compiled IR, packed and nested alternatives. Pending compilation is temporary availability; AUTO can use legal interpreted work without training compiled-arm runtime evidence from it.

Replace blanket specialist precedence with comparable costs for scans versus distinct terms, correlated reopening, compressed versus expanded multiplicity, memory/merge/spill/sort, compilation readiness and parallel overhead. Validate blank-node, distinct-subject and low-multiplicity grouped counts together; do not simply remove guards that currently protect distinct-subject counting.

Share reducer legality with packed/factorised-tail execution. Add SAMPLE/DISTINCT/GROUP_CONCAT where weights, order and separability are proved. Retain natural no-fusion, nonseparable-tail, unknown-custom-merge and unsafe-effect exclusions. Report filtering, packed dispatch and actual compression separately. Explain and runtime opening use the same capability checks and distinguish structural incompatibility, missing binding, compile pending, memory refusal, cancellation and actual per-boundary execution.

### Milestone 7: Integration and full-data acceptance


Re-explain and execute all twenty selected queries plus supplemental GROUP_CONCAT. Classify every earlier decline honestly. Use isolated copies of the stopped store with controlled initial calibration and never overwrite original results. Keep canonical text, HTTP protocol, complete output consumption and timing definitions unchanged.

Run one fresh-JVM suite and three warm suites with ready accelerators, sketches disabled, profiling off, and the same host/resources. Every AUTO query must finish within90seconds in every suite while the configured timeout remains180seconds. Use three interleaved AUTO/alternative pairs on investigated queries; warm AUTO should be within10percent of the best correctly executed applicable strategy measured by that protocol. Verify complete results and actual routes/workers/compression, treating SAMPLE membership and unordered topK ties semantically. Expensive forced routes may time out but must cancel without unbounded work or ownership leaks. Fix failing milestones or retain correct fallbacks; never alter semantics or acceptance to obtain a pass.

## Concrete Steps


Run all commands from the repository root. At execution start run the prescribed root clean install, offline first, capturing full output in maven-build.log and filtering only warnings/summary as required by AGENTS.md. Never overlap raw Maven with cooperative workspaces. Use a background process/session for long jobs; worker owns execution, polling and recovery end-to-end.

    mvn -B -ntp -Dmaven.compiler.showWarnings=false -T 1C -o -Dmaven.repo.local=.m2_repo -Pquick clean install

Use the repository's required tee/awk logging wrapper around that command. If missing dependencies/plugins prevent offline resolution, retry exactly once online then return offline. For another build failure retry without -T1C, then diagnose rather than repeat blindly.

For each behavior change add and observe a smallest-scope failing in-repo test first. Prefer the existing test class that owns the contract, e.g.:

    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py TimeLimitIterationCancellationTest --retain-logs
    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py LmdbQueryExecutionDeadlineTest --module core/sail/lmdb --retain-logs
    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py LmdbNativeScalarPlanTest --module core/sail/lmdb --retain-logs

Narrow those selections to the new method when possible. Persist first red reports before reruns overwrite them, append traceable evidence to initial-evidence.txt and retain milestone-specific logs under a new implementation evidence directory. Post command, report path and1-30 actual summary lines at milestone handoff. After production fix rerun the identical focused selection, relevant neighboring tests and affected modules. Never use -am or -q when tests execute.

    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py core/common/iterator --retain-logs
    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py core/repository/sail --retain-logs
    python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py core/sail/lmdb --retain-logs

New/edited Java files need exact project copyright/SPDX headers and the Codex signature without changing existing copyright years. Run scripts/checkCopyrightPresent.sh before the prescribed formatter. Formatting is serialized; use the required workspace-local Maven repository. Keep generated changes scoped and inspect diff/status after formatting.

## Validation and Acceptance


Test arbitrary scalar nesting, Unicode, languages/datatypes, unbound/error and effectful functions; correlated EXISTS/NOT EXISTS with UNION/MINUS/OPTIONAL/VALUES/BIND/filters/subqueries/contexts; empty/error-only groups, duplicate weights, exact DISTINCT/SAMPLE/GROUP_CONCAT ordering and separators, numeric edge cases and computed values; tiny budgets/skew/spills; early close, worker activation/submission failure, snapshot changes, cancellation races and reclamation; and cold/warm compilation/arbitration/telemetry.

Use deterministic latches and controlled clocks, not sleeps that hide flakiness. Compare generic/reference semantics on small diverse fixtures and full stored responses where deterministic. Independently validate SAMPLE membership and topK ties. Check genuine parallelism and factorisation at actual execution boundaries; preview or requested labels alone are insufficient.

The original HTTP baseline104successes do not prove correctness against the published peer graph, whose snapshot differs. Original full-data same-store evidence and independent semantic tests are the relevant oracle. Recompute derived statistics with sorted/statistics.median, verify against raw arrays, and preserve raw files/hashes.

No new dependency, public tuning switch, authoritative store format or migration is planned. Existing execution policy governs new capabilities. Cancellation errors retain correct precedence and remain QueryInterruptedException to callers. Censored/partial/probe/truncated work must not train completed-runtime calibration.

## Idempotence and Recovery


Keep the canonical dataset/store/results and every unrelated untracked artifact. Do not broadly stage, reset, clean, stash, delete or restore user work. Never run competing store processes or timing workloads. Preserve logs/caches and recover a worker's failed operational workflow within its ownership. At every handoff state source locks, Maven ownership, live sessions, current revision and incomplete gates. Do not change branch or push without user authorization. Use existing GH prefix if committing; otherwise GH-0000 per repository convention, and document that no issue number was supplied.

## Interfaces and Dependencies


Use existing QueryExecutionDeadline.onExpiration/Registration and native cancellation/worker scopes. Add only internal expression compilation callbacks, immutable predicate/reducer capability descriptors, NativeTermDomainPlan and aggregate-result demands. Descriptor behavior must be shared across compilation, explain, admission and runtime binding. Resource owners outlive accepted tasks and own all registrations, IDs/payload spools and reservations. Reuse JDK/current project facilities and existing spill encoding foundations; no additional library is authorized by this plan.

## Artifacts and Notes


Original baseline and investigation artifacts remain authoritative historical evidence and are never rewritten to claim a fix. New implementation evidence must identify source/JAR hashes, effective config, query hashes, model state, raw phase/timing/results, route certifications and complete acceptance outcomes. Final delivery includes implemented scope, red/green reports, the updated capability matrix, per-timeout cause evidence, controlled comparison and all105-query results for each acceptance suite. Any unfinished accepted capability or performance criterion remains explicitly outstanding.

Revision 2026-10-25 (snapshot185, Claude Code): the merged source (upstream main 7f1f2d9dbb and the pushed develop integration 0a3e4f4ea7 merged into fd5d473b91 plus the snapshot182 checkpoint) passes the combined 32-class gate with 515 tests, zero failures and zero errors after four corrections. (1) LmdbNativeAdaptiveArbitration.normalEligible keeps complete page/root-domain algorithms (adjacencyCensus, run-count histogram, datatype histogram, predicate plane groups; LmdbNativeStrategyPreference.completeDomainAlgorithmFamily) in the normal dispatch frontier: their applicability is a structural proof of bounded metadata work while their DISTINCT-domain or cross-group feature quote stays explicitly unknown, so the three Census AUTO regressions select adjacencyCensus again; a new arbiter control proves an ordinary arm with unknown work is still only a fallback. The per-feature Census cost witness remains open work. (2) NativeAggregateCostQuotes no longer conserves the proposal's direct-timing placeholder reason ("the selected executable has no complete physical work quote") into the physical quote that is itself that quote; genuine legacy refusals (mapped origins, declined producers) are still conserved. This restores finite known quotes for the zero-key outer nestedLoop over a lexical transfer, the parsed closed CONCAT receipt and the selected summary's outer SUM proposal. (3) Four test contracts superseded by the source180 owned-work policy were migrated, each asserting the new behaviour precisely: mixedProposalSetRetainsStaticIntervalEvidence now expects the complete nestedLoop ahead of the UNKNOWN-work arm (which remains a fallback) while the statically absurd irAggregate still loses; the decision-diagnostics OBSERVATION event records trainingAccepted=false with the "neither measured nor independently owned" reason for a drain that reports no counter; the parallel IR GROUP_CONCAT quote and the metadata FULL event-retention control expect physicalFeaturesKnown=false with a GROUP_CONCAT missing-work proof and the bounded sort still present in partialEstimate. (4) selectedTelemetryPublishesFrozenQuotesOnlyAtActualOpening's RDF-iteration branch now selects an RDF-term COUNT DISTINCT recipe; a LEXICAL_LENGTH summary is never legal at an RDF result boundary, so that branch had been a fixture defect. Evidence: /private/tmp/rdf4j-gh6205-snapshot184/ (focused reds and greens) and /private/tmp/rdf4j-gh6205-snapshot185/gate/ (515/0/0, formatter and header check exit 0). Full-module, Failsafe, HTTP qualification and the 105x4 acceptance remain outstanding. Next: SPARQLoscope performance on the native engine with adjacency enabled, starting from the snapshot176 per-query data: group-by-string-groupconcat (60) times out, number-of-objects (103) timed out under the 131 runtime while source148+ completes it natively in about 5 s, group-by-count-object-low-multiplicity (49) runs 124-165 s against a 14 s baseline, blank-node count (105) drifts warm from 2.3 s to 22 s, and the string MIN/MAX and regex/str family sit at 15-48 s.

Revision 2026-10-25 (snapshots 186-192, Claude Code): first SPARQLoscope performance round on the native engine with adjacency enabled, measured through the strategy harness against an APFS clone of the 503,865,752-triple format-6 DBLP store under /private/tmp/rdf4j-gh6205-perf (controlled cost model restored before each JVM, loaded host, native API, no HTTP; diagnostic timings, not acceptance). Baseline on the current source before this round, cold: number-of-objects 0.34 s (the 131 runtime timed out), number-of-blank-nodes 3.9 s, group-by-count-object-low-multiplicity 88.6 s, group-by-implicit-string-min 36.9 s, group-by-string-groupconcat 87.9 s (completes with the exact sum 369364948 through the serial lexical-length typed route). Profiles attributed query 49 to the adjacency count emitting 52,069,157 groups as RDF binding sets that were spilled as lexical strings, read back and fully sorted for ORDER BY DESC(?count) LIMIT 10, with per-row ThreadLocal probe-deadline polls on top. Changes: (1) NativeRootPipeline fuses ORDER BY directly under a bounded LIMIT (offset+limit at most 2^20) into a NativeTopKStep: a heap of the retained rows with a sequence tie-break reproduces exactly the first offset+limit rows of the stable complete sort, xsd:integer keys compare as longs, and a slice without LIMIT still sorts fully; LmdbNativeAggregateCompiler.TOP_K_ROOTS counts fused roots and the plan reads TopK(offset, limit, keys). (2) NativeResultReplay.resolvingStoreTermsWith(source) lets NativeSpillValueCodec spill terms of the evaluation's dictionary (including through a synthetic evaluation wrapper's backing source, revision id learned from one probe lookup) as TAG_STORE_TERM plus the ID, replayed as the source's lazy term, and canonical-label xsd:integer literals as TAG_INTEGER_LITERAL plus a long; foreign or other-revision terms keep the exact lexical codec. NativeReplayBindingList, its copy path and the left-join replay pass their sources. (3) The replay list, replay cursor and append path poll cancellation and the probe deadline through the masked poll(tick) contract instead of every row; the spill codec deliberately keeps its per-chunk check (NativeCoordinatorCompletionContractTest). (4) VectorPageCodec copies overlay record bytes with MemorySegment.copy instead of toArray, whose per-call bounds message formatted the carrier class name. Measured cold, same host: query 49 88.6 s to 74.8 s (top-K) to 49.5 s (ID spill) to 36.2 s (polling and overlay copy); query 51 17.6 s to 15.6 s; query 58 37-46 s to 27.0 s; query 60 87.9 s to 76.7 s; number-of-blank-nodes 3.9 s to 2.95 s; number-of-objects 0.03 s warm-store cold-JVM. Query 49's remaining cost is the adjacency count's per-group QueryBindingSet, lazy value and BigInteger literal materialization into the bounded replay; a typed (ID plus primitive count) group output consumed by a native top-K is the next step, and the GROUP_CONCAT route still materializes every lexical value to measure its length (a header-level code-point length read through the value overlay is the planned follow-up).

The first CI run of the merged head (ba2820bc18) failed 25 tests. Locally reproduced and corrected: the per-graph census smoke integration test timed out because the Census grouped recipe, newly admitted by the private work and kept in the normal frontier by the snapshot185 eligibility clause, is input-sized per-group hashing on 14.6M quads; LmdbNativeCensusAggregate now reports KeyProperties(HASHED, requiresHashing) for grouped recipes without a physical quote, such variants never qualify as bounded domain algorithms, and an unquoted hashing domain algorithm is the last arm a cold inventory opens. The six prefix-run DISTINCT routes, three wildcard predicate batch routes and the WCOJ cyclic route were starved by the source180 rule that demoted every unknown-work arm behind any static-complete arm; LmdbNativeStrategyPreference.structuralSpecialistFamily (bounded specialists plus prefixRun, prefixRunGroups, orderedDistinct, orderedDistinctGroups, orderedSinglePatternGroups) now counts structural applicability as the resource proof of a cold normal choice unless the variant requires hashing. An attempted stricter rule (only producer-certified quotes, anchors or completions qualify) broke three source180 arbiter contracts and ten prefix-run aggregate routes and was withdrawn. Three LmdbNativeAdaptiveCostModelTest contracts migrated to truthful feature facts (produced rows measure OUTPUT_MATERIALIZATION only, unmeasured features are never bridged from the estimate, direct timing is retained without a machine sample), and KernelAdjacencyCursorTest now expects the actual per-quad neighbour copies (5) the page batches perform. The complete LMDB module unit gate passes: 10,344 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 34 skipped (snapshot192); the smoke integration test passes in 36 s; LmdbReadAttemptCancellationTest and LmdbRealEstateNativeParityTest, which failed on CI, pass locally and are watched on the next CI run. Evidence under /private/tmp/rdf4j-gh6205-snapshot186 through 192 and /private/tmp/rdf4j-gh6205-perf/run1 through run5.

Revision 2026-10-25 (snapshot193, Claude Code): the second CI run (head 3d030e23ba) left seven unit failures, all CI-only. Six are LmdbReadAttemptCancellationTest.finalizationCancelsAdmittedNativeWaits cases: the finalizing thread was still inside AbstractSailConnection.forceCloseActiveOperations, which runs up to ten System.gc() passes over whatever the shared module test JVM retains before the connection reaches its active iteration, so on the loaded runner the test's two-second native-exit budget expired before the cancellation was even requested (native thread not interrupted in the captured stacks). TxnManager, the lock manager and that test are byte-identical to main; the test passes locally 24/24 even at -XX:ActiveProcessorCount=2. Its budget is now 30 seconds, bounding a hang rather than asserting a latency the close path never guaranteed. The seventh, LmdbNativeGeneratedQueryCoverageTest.lexicalBoundariesRemainNativeWithGenericIslandsDisabled, treated an irKernel COMPILE_PENDING decline as a capability refusal; a pending Janino compilation on a slow host is a timing state (the interpreted tier was outranked and the row ran natively), so it now counts as non-capability. Both classes plus the pipeline and replay tests pass locally (75/0/0).
